Top Songs of 2022

The top songs of 2022 reflected a year of genre fusion, viral discovery, and global impact. As streaming and TikTok continued to dominate music discovery, artists across pop, hip-hop, R&B, Latin, and electronic music found new ways to connect with audiences. From stadium anthems to dancefloor revival, 2022 marked a turning point where nostalgic sounds collided with fresh innovation—and both longtime superstars and breakout talents found massive success.

With chart-toppers from Harry Styles, Beyoncé, Bad Bunny, and Taylor Swift, alongside viral sensations from Steve Lacy, Latto, and GAYLE, the 2022 songs captured a bold, transitional year in music. Whether you’re revisiting 2022 pop songs or exploring them for the first time, this archive provides the complete weekly Top 40 rankings from one of the most vibrant years in modern music.

How We Ranked the Top Songs of 2022

*Note: Based on the Nolan Method, these are the year-end best songs of 2022 that represented the Top 40 songs during the calendar year 2022. The Nolan Method ranking system was formulated by lead Top40Weekly contributor Jarrett Nolan and is based on a point system for an artist’s chart performance on Billboard charts.

Notable Songs & Artists from 2022

With a mix of timeless pop melodies, innovative genre mashups, and global viral success, the top songs of 2022 showcased a music industry embracing both tradition and transformation—and set the stage for a future driven by creativity and connection.

Harry Styles & the Global Reign of “As It Was”

One of the most defining tracks of the year, Harry Styles’ “As It Was” topped the Billboard Hot 100 for 15 weeks, becoming one of the longest-running No. 1 singles in history. With its nostalgic synth-pop vibe and deeply personal lyrics, the song resonated across generations, securing Styles’ place as one of the decade’s leading pop artists.

Beyoncé’s Renaissance & the Revival of Dance

Beyoncé returned with Renaissance, a bold, club-inspired album that paid homage to ’90s house, ballroom, and disco. “Break My Soul” became an empowerment anthem, celebrating resilience and joy. The album’s celebration of Black queer culture redefined pop in 2022 and earned widespread critical acclaim, with Beyoncé pushing sonic boundaries once again.

Bad Bunny Breaks Barriers

No artist had a greater global impact in 2022 than Bad Bunny. His album Un Verano Sin Ti became the most-streamed album of the year, producing hit after hit—including “Tití Me Preguntó”, “Me Porto Bonito”, and “Ojitos Lindos.” Blending reggaetón, trap, and Caribbean sounds, he brought Latin music to the top of global charts and proved its dominance in the streaming era.

Viral Hits & the TikTok Effect

Social media, especially TikTok, drove many of the year’s biggest breakout moments. Steve Lacy’s “Bad Habit” exploded into a No. 1 hit thanks to viral snippets, while GAYLE’s “abcdefu” became a rebellious pop-punk anthem that ruled user-generated content. TikTok also helped Joji’s emotional ballad “Glimpse of Us” and Nicky Youre’s feel-good track “Sunroof” reach mainstream attention.

Hip-Hop’s Shifting Sound & Star Power

Hip-hop continued to evolve, with Drake and 21 Savage releasing the collaborative album Her Loss, while Kendrick Lamar made a powerful return with “The Heart Part 5” and his introspective album Mr. Morale & the Big Steppers. Lizzo delivered one of the year’s biggest feel-good hits with “About Damn Time,” blending funk, empowerment, and infectious energy. Latto also made a mark with “Big Energy,” one of the most-played rap-pop crossovers of the year.

More 2022 Pop Songs
